   a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return np.inf

Target profiles:
```
def _target_profiles(L, prev_b_norm):
  t=np.linspace(0,1,L)
  res=[]
  res.append(ones)
  edge=t*(1-t)
  for d in [0.5,1,2]: res.append(1 - 0.8* edge**d); # endpoints lower
  for s in [0.2,0.5,1]: res.append(1 + s*(2t-1)) # skew positive clip
  for c in [-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.5]: res.append(1 + c*np.cos(2*np.pi*(2t-1)))
  if prev_b_norm: blend ff*prev_b_norm + (1-ff)*ones
```

def _lp_iterate(f, max_seconds, n_lp=1024):
  f=norm(f)
  best=f; best_sc=score(f)
  while time...
    M=score(f)/(2n)  # since sum1
    M=maxb from fft? We can compute b max and M.
    g=_lp_complement(f, M, time_limit=20)
    if g is None: break
    # rescale g sum1
    improved=False
    for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
       cand=(1-t)*f + t*g; cand=norm; sc=score
       if sc<best_sc: best=cand; best_sc; improved; f=cand? maybe set to best
    if not improved: try lower t? break
  return best,best_sc
```

`_refine(a, deadline, seed=0, lr0=0.35)`:

def _refine(a_init, deadline, seed=1):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=a_init.size; a=norm(a_init)
    v=np.log(a+1e-300); v-=v.mean(); v=np.clip(v, -20,20)
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1); L=2*n-1
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fft(a)
    cur_sc=best_sc; cur_v=v.copy(); cur_a=a.copy()
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    stagnation_iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       elapsed=...; progress=...
       # occasional random kick if stagnation
       if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_time (e.g., min(45, 0.2*alloc)):
          v = cur_v + rng.normal(0, 0.2*(1-progress), n); v-=mean; clip; continue
       a = softmax(v)
       F=np.fft.fft(a,m); b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]; b=max(b,0)
       M=float(b.max())
       sc=2*n*M
       if sc < cur_sc: cur_sc=sc
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-10: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=now
       # if b max too large? 
       # weights
       # quantile: choose top_frac 0.12 early -> 0.03 late
       top_frac = 0.12*(1-progress)+0.03
       k_active = max(4, int(round(top_frac * L)))
       # We need threshold. Instead of sort each iter, use argpartition to get top k max value; tau = max(M - b_topk.min()*0.5, 1e-9*M?)
       if k_active < L:
          idx = np.argpartition(b, L-k_active)[L-k_active:]
          b_top_min = b[idx].min()
          delta = M - b_top_min
          if delta < 1e-12 + 1e-6*M: delta = max(1e-6*M, 1e-20)
       else: delta=1e-3*M
       # Maybe add second smaller delta: tau=max(delta, 0.01*M)
       tau=max(delta, 0.005*M)
       x=(b-M)/tau
       x=np.clip(x, -700, 0)
       w=np.exp(x)
       # add small floor? w += 1e-4? no.
       # top boost
       top_k = max(2, int(0.02*max(1,L)))
       idx2 = np.argpartition(b, L-top_k)[L-top_k:]
       w[idx2] += 1.0
       ws=w.sum(); if ws<=0: w=M?; w/=ws
       c=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(w,m)*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
       cm=np.dot(c,a)
       gv=a*(c-cm)
       gnorm=np.sqrt(np.mean(gv*gv))
       if not finite or gnorm < 1e-20:
          # random
          v += rng.normal(0,0.1,n); v-=mean; clip; continue
       gv/=gnorm
       t+=1
       mu=...; nu=...
       mhat=...; vhat=...
       lr = 0.45*(1-0.7*progress)
       base_step = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       # line search
       accepted=False
       for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
          v_try = cur_v - lr*fac*base_step
          v_try -= v_try.mean(); v_try=np.clip(v_try, -20,20)
          a_try=softmax(v_try)
          sc_try=score_fft_simple(a_try,m) # use cache? We'll write helper taking m.
          if sc_try < cur_sc - 1e-12:
             cur_v=v_try; cur_a=a_try; cur_sc=sc_try; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          # maybe keep small step? Could reject, stagnation.
          # update v to previous? cur_v unchanged base_step from old v; need recompute?
          # But v variable may have been updated? We'll maintain cur_v as current. Use cur_v for computations above? We used a=softmax(v) but should use cur_v. Let's set v=cur_v at loop start. If no accept, leave cur_v.
          stagnation_iter...
```

Detailed helper implementations:

`_softmax(v)`:
```
def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0/v.size)
    return e/s
```

`_log_plus(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,1e-300); return np.log(a)
```

`_norm(a)`:
```
def _norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    return a/s
```

`_score_fft(a)` no m:
```
def _score_fft(a):
  a=_norm(a); if None return inf
  n=a.size; m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
  return _score_fft_m(a,m,n)
```
`_score_fft_m(a,m,n)` assumes sum1:
```
F=np.fft.fft(a,m); b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]; if not finite return inf; b=np.maximum(b,0); M=b.max(); return 2*n*M
```
Could pass `n` global. For speed in line search, use a helper `_trial_score(v,m)`? Too many.

In `_refine`, line search calls `_score_fft_m`, which allocates. Fine.

Parametric starts:
```
def _param_inits(n, rng, prev):
  out=[]
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  if prev is not None: out.append(_interp(prev,n))
  # uniform
  out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
  # polynomial powers
  tt=np.clip(t, 1e-6, 1-1e-6)
  for q in (-0.5,0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
    for p in (-0.5,0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
       a=tt**q * (1.0-tt)**p
       # maybe random phase
       an=_norm(a); if an is not None out.append(an)
  # linear skew
  for c in (-0.9,-0.7,-0.5,-0.3,0.2,0.5):
     a=1.0 + c*(t*2-1.0)? Ensure nonneg? For c=-0.9, min 0.1 ok. Include c up to 2 maybe min -3 negative but clip in norm? If negative large sequence shape changes; better ensure nonnegative. Use a=1 + c*(2t-1); if min<0 then a=np.maximum(a,0) maybe.
  # sine/cos modulation
  for base in [np.ones(n), np.sqrt(tt*(1-tt))+1e-3, tt**(0.5)*(1-tt)**(0.5)?]:
     for freq in (1,2,3): amp in (0.1,0.3,0.5): a=base*(1+amp*np.cos(2*pi*freq*(t-0.5)+rng...)); norm
  return out
```
Too many starts maybe 50+; score each O(n log n). For n=8192, 100 starts * FFT ~ maybe 100*0.01s=1s okay. For 16k maybe 2s. Limit per stage to e.g., 60.

We can reduce powers: q/p values 4x4=16. Good.

Spectral starts function:
```
def _spectral_inits(n, rng, prev_b=None):
   L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,L)
   targets=[]
   targets.append(np.ones(L))
   edge=t*(1-t)
   for d in (1.0,1.5,2.0):
      targets.append(1.0 - 1.2*edge**d) # endpoints lower maybe negative clip
   for s in (-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.5):
      targets.append(1.0 + s*(2*t-1.0) + 0.2*t*(1-t))
   for amp in (0.2,0.5):
      targets.append(1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*(2*t-1.0)))
   if prev_b is not None:
      for ff in (0.3,0.6,0.9): targets.append(ff*prev_b + (1-ff)*np.ones(L))
   out=[]
   for target in targets:
      target=np.maximum(target,0.0); tm=target.max(); if tm>0: target/=tm
      for init in [None, _norm(np.linspace(1,0.1,n)), _norm(np.linspace(0.1,1,n))]:
         a,sc=_spectral_factor_target(target,n,m,init,iters=12)
         if a is not None: out.append(a)
   return out
```
This could be heavy: for n=8192, targets ~12*3 inits*12 iters*FFT m=16384 ~ 400 FFTs ~0.4s? okay. For all stages maybe okay. But previous code had spectral stage with 6 runs. We can limit: only generate spectral inits for n≤4096? Or max 8 candidates. To save time, produce e.g., 12 targets but 1 init each (best current). We can pass best_a as init for each; no multiple inits.

`_spectral_factor_target` implementation:
```
def _spectral_factor_target(target, n, m, init=None, iters=20, blend=0.5):
  L=2*n-1
  target=np.asarray(target,float)
  if target.size<L: pad; else target=target[:L]
  target=np.maximum(target,0.0); tm=target.max(); if tm<=0: target=np.ones(L)
  else target/=tm
  T=np.fft.fft(target,m)
  if init is None:
     a=np.real(np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)))[:n]
     if a.max()<=0: a=ones
  else: a=_norm(init)
  a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=a.sum()
  best=a; best_sc=_score_fft(a)
  Tnorm=max(abs(T)) +1e-300
  for it in range(iters):
     F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
     eps=1e-8*max(1.0, np.max(np.abs(F)))
     # avoid zero: F_safe = F.copy(); zero mask absF<eps -> eps
     idx=np.abs(F)<eps; F_safe=F.copy(); F_safe[idx]=eps
     Fnew=0.5*(F_safe + T/F_safe)
     anew=np.real(np.fft.ifft(Fnew))[:n]
     pos=np.maximum(anew,0.0)
     ps=pos.sum()
     if not finite or ps<1e-12:
        pos=a
     bld=max(0.2, blend*(1 - 0.5*it/iters))
     a_new=(1-bld)*a + bld*pos
     a_new=np.maximum(a_new,1e-12); a_new/=a_new.sum()
     if not finite: a_new=a
     a=a_new
     if (it+1)%5==0:
        sc=_score_fft(a); if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
  sc=_score_fft(a); if sc<best_sc: best_sc; best=a
  return _norm(best), float(best_sc)
```
The fixed point might diverge; blend helps. It may not improve but okay. Use iters=10 to save.

Need `_score_fft(a)` inside many spectral inits could be expensive; use `_score_fft`. Fine.

LP function global pattern cache:
```
_LP_CACHE = {}
def _lp_model(f):
    n=f.size; L=2*n-1
    if n not in _LP_CACHE:
        dt=np.int32
        base=np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=dt), n)
        base += np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=dt), n)
        indptr=np.arange(n+1, dtype=np.int64)*n
        _LP_CACHE[n]=(base, indptr)
    return _LP_CACHE[n]
```
But `np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)` creates int32; adding tile may overflow int32? n^2 max for n=2048 4M <2^31; tile values n 2048; row indices <4095, no overflow. If n up to 65535, n^2 4e9 overflow int32; we limit. Good.

But `base = np.repeat...; base += np.tile...` may allocate tile. We can do:
`rows = np.empty(n*n, dtype=dt); np.copyto(rows, np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=dt), n)); rows += np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=dt), n)`. Memory peak two arrays. Simpler okay for 2048.

Potential issue: `np.csc_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(L,n))` for scipy sparse version expects data length=(indptr[-1]). Yes. `indices` row indices within 0..L-1. For column n-1, rows n-1 .. n-1+n-1=2n-2 =L-1, ok. If repeated for all columns, column i has rows i..i+n-1, yes.

LP call:
```
def _lp_complement(f, M, time_limit=20.0):
   if not _HAVE_SCIPY: return None
   n=f.size
   if n > 2048: return None
   if M <=0 or not finite: return None
   f_work=f*float(n)
   M_work=M*float(n)
   indices, indptr = _lp_model(n)
   data=np.repeat(f_work, n)
   A=_sp.csc_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(2*n-1,n))
   c=-np.ones(n)
   b=np.full(2*n-1, M_work)
   bounds=[(0.0,None) for _ in range(n)]  # lambda? no, list comprehension is fine not lambda? They said no lambda functions; list comprehension okay.
   opts={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': float(time_limit)}
   try:
      res=_linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
   except Exception:
      try: res=_linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
      except Exception: return None
   if res is None: return None
   x=getattr(res,'x',None)
   if x is None: return None
   g=np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
   if g.size!=n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return None
   g=np.maximum(g,0)
   s=g.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   return g/s
```
List comprehension okay (not lambda). We could avoid bounds arg to reduce memory? Passing bounds=None means default variable bounds [0, inf]? SciPy linprog bounds default is (None, None) unbounded? Actually if bounds=None, variables unbounded. We need nonnegative. Use `bounds=(0.0, None)` scalar? SciPy accepts scalar? It accepts sequence or scalar? If scalar (0,None) might broadcast? I think bounds can be (lb, ub) scalar? Docs: If None, all unbounded; if scalar? maybe. Safer use `bounds=[(0, None)]*n` (list multiplication okay no lambda). Use that.

Linprog with sparse A csc and dense c. It may convert. Time.

def _lp_stage(a_init, n, time_limit_total):
   f=_norm(a_init)
   if n>2048: # downsample? Instead use length min(n,2048)
      f2=_interp(f, 2048) maybe if n>2048
   else f2=f
   best=f2; best_sc=_score_fft(f2)
   deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit_total
   iters=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline and iters<8:
      sc=best_sc; M=sc/(2*best.size)
      g=_lp_complement(best, M, time_limit=15.0)
      if g is None: break
      improved=False
      for mix in (1.0,0.8,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05):
         cand=(1-mix)*best + mix*g
         cand=_norm(cand)
         if cand is None: continue
         sc2=_score_fft(cand)
         if sc2<best_sc:
            best_sc=sc2; best=cand; improved=True; break
         elif mix==1.0: # track best mix
            pass
      if not improved:
         # try reversed g
         gr=g[::-1]
         for mix in (0.5,0.8,0.2,1.0): ...
      if not improved: break
      iters+=1
   return best, best_sc
```

def _lp_improve(a_init, total_time):

    lp_n = min(orig_n, 2048)

Pseudo code final:
```
def _refine(a_init, deadline, seed=1234):
    a=_norm(a_init)
    if a is None: return None, inf
    n=a.size
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    L=2*n-1
    v=_log_plus(a); v -= v.mean(); v=np.clip(v, -24,24)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fft_m(a,m,n)
    v_best=v.copy(); sc_best=best_sc
    cur_v=v.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    stuck_time = min(45.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.monotonic())*0.1))
    n_iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       elapsed=max(1e-9, deadline - start?) need start.
```
Need `start` inside: `start=time.monotonic(); total= max(1.0, deadline-start)`.
```
    progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/total)
```
In loop, use current time. If stuck:
```
       if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_time:
          sigma = 0.20*(1.0-progress)+0.03
          if rng.random()<0.4:
             v = _log_plus(best_a) - max; clip
          else:
             v = cur_v + rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
          v -= mean; clip
          cur_v = v.copy(); cur_sc = _score_fft_m(_softmax(v),m,n)
          last_improve=now
          continue
```
Avoid using variable v for cur and scratch. Let's maintain `v = cur_v.copy()` at loop start.

`_parse_prev`:
```
def _prev_sequence():
    try:
        cand = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if cand is None: return None
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0: return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr,0.0)
        s=arr.sum()
        if s <= 0: return None
        return arr/s
    except Exception:
        return None
```

`_interp` handles if a None.
```
def _interp(a, n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    a=_norm(a)
    if a is None: return np.ones(n)/float(n)
    if a.size==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: out.fill(1/n)
    else: out/=s
    return out
```
`_interp` calls `_norm`, which defined before. Function order doesn't matter.

`_score_fft_m`:
```
def _score_fft_m(a, m, n):
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)): return np.inf
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): return np.inf
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    M=float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <=0: return np.inf if M<=0 else 0? If M=0 possible all zero; not.
    return float(2.0*n*M)
```
If M=0 due all zero, return inf.

```
def _score_fft(a):
    a=_norm(a)
    if a is None: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    return _score_fft_m(a,m,n)

def _b_normalized(a):
    a=_norm(a)
    if a is None: return None
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m); b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]; b=np.maximum(b,0)
    bm=b.max()
    if bm<=0 or not finite: return None
    return b/bm
```

def _spectral_inits(n, rng, best_a, max_count=10):
   starts=[]
   L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,L)
   targets=[]
   targets.append(np.ones(L))
   edge=t*(1-t)
   for d in (1.0,1.5,2.0):
       targets.append(1.0 - 1.1*edge**d)
   for s in (-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.5,0.8):
       targets.append(1.0 + s*(2*t-1.0) + 0.2*edge)
   for amp in (0.3,0.6):
       targets.append(1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*(2*t-1.0)))
   prev_b = _b_normalized(best_a) if best_a is not None else None
   if prev_b is not None:
       for ff in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
           targets.append(ff*prev_b + (1.0-ff)*np.ones(L))
   # limit number targets
   if len(targets)>12: targets=targets[:12]
   init=best_a if best_a is not None else np.ones(n)
   for target in targets:
       target=np.asarray(target,dtype=float)
       if target.size<L:
           tmp=np.zeros(L); tmp[:target.size]=target; target=tmp
       else: target=target[:L]
       target=np.maximum(target,0)
       tm=target.max()
       if tm<=0 or not finite: target=np.ones(L)
       else: target/=tm
       for it in (6,10):
          a,sc=_spectral_factor_target(target,n,m,init, iters=it, blend=0.45)
          if a is not None:
             starts.append(a)
             if len(starts)>=max_count*2: break
       if len(starts)>=max_count*2: break
   return starts
```
Maybe too many spectral factor calls (12*2=24) with 10 iters each. For n=4096 m=8192, 24*10 FFT ~240 FFT, ok. For all stages maybe. Limit max_count 6. We'll set max_count=6.

def _lp_improve(a_init, total_time):
    orig_n=int(a_init.size)
    lp_n=min(orig_n, 2048)
    f=_interp(_norm(a_init), lp_n)
    if f is None: return None, inf
    best=f; best_sc=_score_fft(f)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+total_time
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()+5 < deadline and it<6:
       M=best_sc/(2*best.size)
       g=_lp_complement(best, M, time_limit=12.0)
       if g is None: break
       improved=False
       for mix in (0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0,0.35,0.1,0.6):
          cand=(1.0-mix)*best + mix*g
          cand=_norm(cand)
          if cand is None: continue
          sc=_score_fft(cand)
          if sc<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best=cand; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          gr=g[::-1]
          for mix in (0.5,0.8,0.2,1.0):
             cand=(1.0-mix)*best + mix*gr
             cand=_norm(cand); if None continue
             sc=_score_fft(cand)
             if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=cand; improved=True; break
       if not improved: break
       it+=1
    if orig_n != lp_n:
       best=_interp(best, orig_n)
       best_sc=_score_fft(best)
    return best, float(best_sc)
```

def _process_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, rng):
    n=int(n)

    else: a0=_interp(_norm(best_a), n)

Let's write `_refine` code with local variables carefully:
```
def _refine(a_init, deadline, seed=1):
    a=_norm(a_init)
    if a is None: return None, np.inf
    n=int(a.size)
    if n < 3: return a, _score_fft(a)
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(1.0, float(deadline)-start)
    fr=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    L=2*n-1
    v=np.log(a + 1e-300)
    v -= float(np.mean(v))
    v=np.clip(v, -24.0, 24.0)
    cur_v=v.copy()
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_sc=_score_fft_m(a,m,n)
    cur_sc=best_sc
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    last_improve=start
    stuck_budget=min(60.0, max(15.0, total*0.12))
    while True:
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 0.5: break
        progress=min(1.0, (now-start)/total)
        if now - last_improve > stuck_budget:
           sigma=0.18*(1.0-progress)+0.02
           if fr.random() < 0.35:
              v_best=np.log(best_a+1e-300)
              v_best-=float(np.mean(v_best)); v_best=np.clip(v_best,-24,24)
              cur_v=v_best.copy()
           else:
              cur_v=cur_v + fr.normal(0, sigma, n)
              cur_v-=float(np.mean(cur_v)); cur_v=np.clip(cur_v,-24,24)
           # set current score
           a_tmp=_softmax(cur_v)
           cur_sc=_score_fft_m(a_tmp,m,n)
           if not np.isfinite(cur_sc): cur_sc=best_sc
           v=cur_v.copy()
           last_improve=now
           continue
        v=cur_v.copy()
        a=_softmax(v)
        F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
        b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            break
        b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
        M=float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: break
        sc=float(2.0*n*M)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=now
           if sc < cur_sc:
              cur_sc=sc
        top_frac=0.10*(1.0-progress)+0.03
        k_act=int(max(4, round(top_frac * L)))
        if k_act < L:
            idx_act=np.argpartition(b, L-k_act)[L-k_act:]
            min_top=float(b[idx_act].min())
            delta=M-min_top
        else:
            delta=0.1*M
        tau=max(delta, 1e-5*M, 1e-300)
        x=(b-M)/tau
        x=np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
        w=np.exp(x)
        k2=int(max(2, round(0.02*L)))
        idx2=np.argpartition(b, L-k2)[L-k2:]
        w[idx2] += 1.0
        ws=float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w=np.zeros(L); w[idx2[-1]] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= ws
        cw=np.fft.fft(w,m)
        c=np.fft.ifft(cw * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        cm=float(np.dot(c,a))
        gv=a*(c-cm)
        gnorm=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv*gv)))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-20:
            cur_v=cur_v + fr.normal(0, 0.05, n)
            cur_v-=float(np.mean(cur_v)); cur_v=np.clip(cur_v,-24,24)
            continue
        gv/=gnorm
        t+=1
        mu=beta1*mu + (1-beta1)*gv
        nu=beta2*nu + (1-beta2)*(gv*gv)
        b1=beta1**t; b2=beta2**t
        mhat=mu/max(1e-12,1-b1)
        bare=gv? no
        vhat=nu/max(1e-12,1-b2)
        step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
        step=np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)
        lr=0.45*(1.0-0.7*progress)
        accepted=False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            v_try = v - lr*fac*step
            v_try -= float(np.mean(v_try))
            v_try = np.clip(v_try, -24.0, 24.0)
            a_try=_softmax(v_try)
            sc_try=_score_fft_m(a_try,m,n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                cur_v=v_try
                cur_sc=sc_try
                if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); last_improve=now
                accepted=True
                break
        if not accepted:
            # Optionally do a very small step if lr very small? no
            pass
    return best_a, float(best_sc)
```

```
def _process_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, rng):
    n=int(n)
    if best_a is None:
       initial=np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       initial=_interp(_norm(best_a), n)
    stage_best=initial; stage_sc=_score_fft(initial)
    starts=[initial]
    starts.extend(_param_inits(n, rng, best_a))
    if n <= 8192 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 60:
       starts.extend(_spectral_inits(n, rng, initial, max_count=6))
    cands=_select_candidates(starts, max_count=5)
    # LP
    rem=stage_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100 and len(cands)>0:
       lp_time=min(rem*0.18, 90.0)
       a_lp, sc_lp=_lp_improve(cands[0], lp_time)
       if a_lp is not None and np.isfinite(sc_lp):
           if sc_lp < stage_sc:
               stage_sc=sc_lp; stage_best=a_lp; cands[0]=a_lp
           elif len(cands) < 5:
               cands.append(a_lp)
    # refine budget
    refine_until=stage_deadline - 1.0
    weights=(0.45,0.25,0.15,0.08,0.07)
    for i,cand in enumerate(cands):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now + 30 > refine_until: break
       rem=refine_until - now
       frac=weights[i] if i < len(weights) else 0.05
       alloc=max(20.0, min(rem, rem*frac + 5.0))
       # ensure remaining total? If first takes rem*0.45+5, leaves rem*0.55 -? okay.
       end=now+alloc
       a_res, sc_res=_refine(cand, end, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31-1)))
       if a_res is not None and np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < stage_sc:
           stage_sc=sc_res; stage_best=a_res
    # final polish if best from LP or cand remains
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now + 30 < refine_until:
       rem=refine_until-now
       a_res, sc_res=_refine(stage_best, now+max(10.0, min(rem, rem*0.6)), seed=int(rng.integers(...)))
       if ... update
    return stage_best, float(stage_sc)
```

Now, code for `_select_candidates`:
```
def _select_candidates(starts, max_count=5):
    scored=[]
    seen=[]
    for a in starts:
       a=_norm(a)
       if a is None: continue
       sc=_score_fft(a)
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       div=True
       for b in seen:
          d=float(np.sum(np.abs(a-b)))
          if d < 0.12:
             div=False; break
       if div:
          scored.append((sc,a)); seen.append(a)
          if len(seen) >= max_count*4: break
    scored.sort(key=_pair_score)
    out=[a for sc,a in scored[:max_count]]
    # Could include all top even if no diverse? if scored shorter, if no candidates, add uniform? propose handles. But if scored empty, return None list.
    return out
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Method:
  - Normalize to sum 1, so objective is 2*n*max convolution coefficient.
  - Use fast FFT convolution for scoring and subgradient/Adam optimization
    in log-softmax coordinates.
  - Generate many diverse starts: previous incumbent interpolation,
    power/beta profiles, skewed polynomials, spectral square-root targets.
  - Optionally use a linear programming complement step with SciPy/HiGHS.
  - Run progressive lengths and keep the best sequence found.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.sparse as _sp
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False
    _sp = None
    _linprog = None

_LP_CACHE = {}


def _pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / s


def _tox(a):
    a = _norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _interp(a, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = _norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out.fill(1.0 / float(n))
    else:
        out /= s
    return out


def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0 / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _score_fft_m(a, m, n):
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    M = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    if M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def _score_fft(a):
    a = _norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    n = int(a.size)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    return _score_fft_m(a, m, n)


def _b_normalized(a):
    a = _norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(L)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bm = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return None
    return b / bm


def _prev_sequence():
    try:
        cand = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if cand is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return None
        return arr / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _param_inits(n, rng, prev):
    outs = []
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if prev is not None:
        p = _interp(prev, n)
        if p is not None:
            outs.append(p)

    outs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    tt = np.clip(t, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
    pows = (-0.5, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0)
    for p in pows:
        left = tt ** p
        for q in pows:
            right = (1.0 - tt) ** q
            a = left * right
            an = _norm(a)
            if an is not None:
                outs.append(an)

    for c in (-0.9, -0.7, -0.5, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6):
        a = 1.0 + c * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        an = _norm(a)
        if an is not None:
            outs.append(an)

    bases = [
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
        np.exp(-4.0 * (t - 0.5) ** 2),
        tt * (1.0 - tt) + 1e-3,
    ]
    for base in bases:
        bn = _norm(base)
        if bn is None:
            continue
        for amp in (0.3, 0.7):
            for k in (1, 2):
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                a = bn * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph))
                an = _norm(a)
                if an is not None:
                    outs.append(an)

    return outs


def _spectral_factor_target(target, n, m, init, iters, blend):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    target = np.asarray(target, dtype=np.float64)
    if target.size < L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:target.size] = target
        target = tmp
    else:
        target = target[:L].copy()
    target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
    tm = float(target.max())
    if not np.isfinite(tm) or tm <= 0.0:
        target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        target /= tm

    T = np.fft.fft(target, m)

    a = _norm(init)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    sq = np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)).real[:n]
    sgn = _norm(sq)
    if sgn is not None:
        a = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * sgn

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = a / float(a.sum())

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft_m(a, m, n)

    for it in range(iters):
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        afmax = float(np.max(np.abs(F)))
        if not np.isfinite(afmax) or afmax <= 0.0:
            afmax = 1.0
        eps = 1e-8 * max(1.0, afmax) + 1e-300
        F_safe = F.copy()
        mask = np.abs(F_safe) < eps
        if np.any(mask):
            F_safe[mask] = eps
        Fnew = 0.5 * (F_safe + T / F_safe)
        anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
        pos = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
        ps = float(pos.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-12:
            pos = a.copy()
        bld = max(0.20, blend * (1.0 - 0.45 * (float(it) / float(max(1, iters)))))
        a_new = (1.0 - bld) * a + bld * pos
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-12)
        snew = float(a_new.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 0.0:
            a_new = a.copy()
        else:
            a_new /= snew
        a = a_new

        if (it + 1) % 4 == 0:
            sc = _score_fft_m(a, m, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()

    sc = _score_fft_m(a, m, n)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best = a.copy()
        best_sc = sc

    best = _norm(best)
    if best is None:
        return None, np.inf
    return best, float(best_sc)


def _spectral_inits(n, rng, best_a, max_count):
    starts = []
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(L)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    edge = t * (1.0 - t)

    targets = []
    targets.append(np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))

    for d in (1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        targets.append(1.0 - 1.1 * (edge ** d))

    for s in (-0.5, -0.2, 0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
        targets.append(1.0 + s * (2.0 * t - 1.0) + 0.2 * edge)

    for amp in (0.3, 0.6):
        targets.append(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (2.0 * t - 1.0)))

    pb = _b_normalized(best_a)
    if pb is not None:
        for ff in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            targets.append(ff * pb + (1.0 - ff) * np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))

    init = best_a
    if init is None:
        init = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    for target in targets:
        if len(starts) >= max_count * 2:
            break
        target = np.asarray(target, dtype=np.float64)
        target = np.where(np.isfinite(target), target, 0.0)
        target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
        tm = float(target.max())
        if not np.isfinite(tm) or tm <= 0.0:
            continue
        target = target / tm
        a, _sc = _spectral_factor_target(target, n, m, init, iters=8, blend=0.45)
        if a is not None:
            starts.append(a)
    return starts


def _select_candidates(starts, max_count):
    scored = []
    seen = []
    for a in starts:
        a = _norm(a)
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = _score_fft(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        diverse = True
        for b in seen:
            d = float(np.sum(np.abs(a - b)))
            if d < 0.12:
                diverse = False
                break
        if diverse:
            scored.append((sc, a))
            seen.append(a)
            if len(seen) >= max_count * 4:
                break

    scored.sort(key=_pair_score)
    out = []
    for _sc, a in scored[:max_count]:
        out.append(a)
    return out


def _lp_model(n):
    if n in _LP_CACHE:
        return _LP_CACHE[n]
    _LP_CACHE.clear()
    dt = np.int32
    base = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=dt), n)
    base += np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=dt), n)
    indptr = np.arange(n + 1, dtype=np.int64) * n
    _LP_CACHE[n] = (base, indptr)
    return _LP_CACHE[n]


def _lp_complement(f, M, time_limit):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 8 or n > 2048:
        return None
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    f_work = f * float(n) + 1e-8
    M_work = M * float(n) + 1e-8

    try:
        indices, indptr = _lp_model(n)
        data = np.repeat(f_work, n)
        A = _sp.csc_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(L, n))
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.full(L, M_work, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        opts = {"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(1.0, time_limit))}
        try:
            res = _linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs", options=opts)
        except Exception:
            res = _linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None:
        return None
    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if g.size != n:
        return None
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return g / s


def _lp_improve(a_init, total_time):
    a_init = _norm(a_init)
    if a_init is None:
        return None, np.inf
    orig_n = int(a_init.size)
    lp_n = min(orig_n, 2048)
    f = _interp(a_init, lp_n)
    if f is None:
        return None, np.inf

    best = f
    best_sc = _score_fft(best)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(total_time)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() + 5.0 < deadline and it < 6:
        M = best_sc / (2.0 * float(best.size))
        g = _lp_complement(best, M, time_limit=12.0)
        if g is None:
            break
        improved = False
        for mix in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 0.35, 0.1, 0.6):
            cand = (1.0 - mix) * best + mix * g
            cand = _norm(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best = cand
                best_sc = sc
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            gr = g[::-1]
            for mix in (0.5, 0.8, 0.2, 1.0):
                cand = (1.0 - mix) * best + mix * gr
                cand = _norm(cand)
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                sc = _score_fft(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best = cand
                    best_sc = sc
                    improved = True
                    break
        if not improved:
            break
        it += 1

    if orig_n != lp_n:
        best = _interp(best, orig_n)
        best_sc = _score_fft(best)
    return best, float(best_sc)


def _refine(a_init, deadline, seed):
    a = _norm(a_init)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 3:
        return a, _score_fft(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(deadline) - start)

    v = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -24.0, 24.0)

    cur_v = v.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(a)
    cur_sc = best_sc

    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    last_improve = start
    stuck_budget = min(60.0, max(15.0, total * 0.12))

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 0.5:
            break
        progress = min(1.0, (now - start) / total)

        if now - last_improve > stuck_budget:
            sigma = 0.18 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.02
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                vb = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
                vb = vb - float(np.mean(vb))
                vb = np.clip(vb, -24.0, 24.0)
                cur_v = vb.copy()
            else:
                cur_v = cur_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                cur_v = cur_v - float(np.mean(cur_v))
                cur_v = np.clip(cur_v, -24.0, 24.0)
            a_tmp = _softmax(cur_v)
            sc_tmp = _score_fft_m(a_tmp, m, n)
            cur_sc = sc_tmp if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) else best_sc
            last_improve = now
            continue

        v = cur_v.copy()
        a = _softmax(v)
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        Mmax = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(Mmax) or Mmax <= 0.0:
            break

        sc = float(2.0 * n * Mmax)
        cur_sc = sc
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_improve = now

        tau_frac = 0.15 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.03
        tau = max(Mmax * tau_frac, 1e-12)

        top_frac = 0.12 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.03
        k_act = int(max(4, round(top_frac * L)))
        if k_act < L:
            idx_act = np.argpartition(b, L - k_act)[L - k_act:]
        else:
            idx_act = np.arange(L, dtype=int)

        x = (b - Mmax) / tau
        x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(x)
        w[idx_act] += 1.0
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(idx_act[0])] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= ws

        W = np.fft.fft(w, m)
        c = np.fft.ifft(W * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        cm = float(np.dot(c, a))
        gv = a * (c - cm)
        gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv * gv)))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-20:
            cur_v = cur_v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
            cur_v = cur_v - float(np.mean(cur_v))
            cur_v = np.clip(cur_v, -24.0, 24.0)
            continue

        gv = gv / gnorm
        t += 1
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * gv
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (gv * gv)
        b1 = beta1 ** t
        b2 = beta2 ** t
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)

        lr = 0.45 * (1.0 - 0.70 * progress)
        accepted = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            v_try = v - (lr * fac * step)
            v_try = v_try - float(np.mean(v_try))
            v_try = np.clip(v_try, -24.0, 24.0)
            a_try = _softmax(v_try)
            sc_try = _score_fft_m(a_try, m, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                cur_v = v_try
                cur_sc = sc_try
                last_improve = now
                if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc_try
                    best_a = a_try.copy()
                accepted = True
                break

        _ = accepted

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def _process_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, rng):
    n = int(n)
    initial = _interp(best_a, n)
    if initial is None:
        initial = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    stage_best = initial
    stage_sc = _score_fft(initial)

    starts = [initial]
    starts.extend(_param_inits(n, rng, best_a))
    if n <= 8192 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 60.0:
        starts.extend(_spectral_inits(n, rng, initial, max_count=6))

    cands = _select_candidates(starts, max_count=5)
    if not cands:
        cands = [initial]

    for cand in cands:
        sc = _score_fft(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < stage_sc:
            stage_sc = sc
            stage_best = cand

    rem = stage_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0:
        lp_time = min(rem * 0.18, 90.0)
        a_lp, sc_lp = _lp_improve(cands[0], lp_time)
        if a_lp is not None and np.isfinite(sc_lp):
            if sc_lp < stage_sc:
                stage_sc = sc_lp
                stage_best = a_lp
                cands[0] = a_lp
            elif len(cands) < 5:
                cands.append(a_lp)

    refine_until = stage_deadline - 1.0
    weights = (0.45, 0.25, 0.15, 0.08, 0.07)

    for i, cand in enumerate(cands):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 30.0 > refine_until:
            break
        rem = refine_until - now
        frac = weights[i] if i < len(weights) else 0.05
        alloc = max(20.0, min(rem, rem * frac + 5.0))
        seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        a_res, sc_res = _refine(cand, now + alloc, seed)
        if a_res is not None and np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < stage_sc:
            stage_sc = sc_res
            stage_best = a_res

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 30.0 < refine_until:
        rem = refine_until - now
        seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        a_res, sc_res = _refine(stage_best, now + max(10.0, min(rem, rem * 0.6)), seed)
        if a_res is not None and np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < stage_sc:
            stage_sc = sc_res
            stage_best = a_res

    return stage_best, float(stage_sc)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return best found sequence of non-negative heights."""
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    start = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    safe = min(240.0, max(60.0, 0.10 * budget_s))
    global_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - safe)
    final_hard_deadline = start + max(120.0, budget_s - 15.0)
    base = max(60.0, budget_s - safe)

    prev = _prev_sequence()
    best_a = prev
    best_score = _score_fft(best_a) if best_a is not None else np.inf
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = 2.0
    print("Initial best score: %.8f len=%d" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    stages = [
        (512, 0.03),
        (1024, 0.08),
        (2048, 0.25),
        (4096, 0.39),
        (8192, 0.25),
    ]

    for n, frac in stages:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 60.0 > global_deadline:
            break
        stage_time = base * frac
        stage_deadline = min(global_deadline - 5.0, now + stage_time)
        print("Stage n=%d budget=%.1fs current=%.8f" % (n, stage_time, best_score))
        a_new, sc_new = _process_stage(int(n), stage_deadline, best_a, rng)
        if a_new is not None and np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_a = a_new
            best_score = sc_new
        print("After stage n=%d best=%.8f len=%d" % (n, best_score, len(best_a) if best_a is not None else 0))

    # Optional final upsample to 16384 if it seems clearly better.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) < 16384 and now < final_hard_deadline - 80.0:
        a_try = _interp(best_a, 16384)
        refine_end = min(final_hard_deadline - 40.0, now + min(120.0, max(30.0, final_hard_deadline - 80.0 - now)))
        a_try, sc_try = _refine(a_try, refine_end, int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
        if a_try is not None and np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score - 1e-6:
            print("Final 16384 upsample candidate FFT score=%.8f" % sc_try)
            try:
                ex = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a_try])
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                    best_a = a_try
                    best_score = float(ex)
                    print("Accepted 16384 length exact score=%.8f" % best_score)
            except Exception as e:
                print("16384 exact check skipped: %s" % e)

    best_a = _norm(best_a)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0
        best_score = 2.0

    # Final validation on small/medium lengths if time permits.
    if time.monotonic() < final_hard_deadline - 10.0 and len(best_a) <= 16384:
        try:
            ex = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
            if np.isfinite(ex):
                print("Final FFT score=%.8f exact score=%.8f len=%d" %
                      (best_score, ex, len(best_a)))
            else:
                best_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0
                best_score = 2.0
        except Exception as e:
            print("Final exact validation skipped: %s" % e)

    out = []
    for x in best_a:
        out.append(float(x))
    return out
```
